## Checking For Doors

The Ker Nethalas map tiles
indicate when a room has doors or
not, but that might not be the case
if you’re using other dungeon tiles
or generators. When this is the
case (or maybe you simply want to
always randomize doors, even
with the oficial Ker Nethalas tiles),
you can use the following table. If
there is a door, follow the rules for
Locks & Traps on page 117.

## D10 Is Therea Door?

1-5 Yes
6-10 No

## Retracing Your Steps

If for some reason you need or
want to go back to an area of the
Domain you’ve already explored,
passing through already cleared
rooms and corridors, you can do
so. You must still roll the Tension
Die each time you enter a room or
corridor, and your lightsource will
be spent as usual (once per room),
but you do not need to check for
Combat Encounters nor roll on the
Events table.
Chapter IV: Exploring Ker Nethalas 99D10 OVERSEER INFLUENCE
1 Tough: Immune to Critical Strikes.
2 Vital: +D6 Health per each 5⅃.
3 Frenzied: +1 damage.
4 Skilled: +10 Combat Skill.
5 Magebane: +10 Magic Resistance.
Resistant: Resistant to 1 random type of damage. Roll on the
6
Damage Type table on page 225.
Corrupting: Each time the creature deals 5+ damage on a single
7
attack, the target must make a Resolve check or lose 1 Sanity.
8 Unstable: Deals 1 Arcane damage when killed.
9 Alert: +20 Mind.
Piercing: Gains the Penetrating (1) Trait. This can stack with other
10
instances of the same Trait.

## D20 Corridor Description

The air is heavy with ancient dust, and the walls are lined with
1
cobwebs. The faint echoes of distant footsteps can be heard.
Flickering torches cast eerie shadows on the stone walls. The air is
2
damp, and the occasional drip of water can be heard.
The tunnel is barely wide enough for a single person to pass
through. The walls are lined with ancient burial niches, some of
3
which have been disturbed. You’re not sure if from within or
without…
Thick moss covers the damp stone walls, giving the corridor an eerie
4
green glow. The sound of water dripping echoes throughout.
The passage is partially blocked by a cave-in, with rubble and debris
5 scattered across the loor. Dim light ilters through cracks in the
ceiling.
The walls are adorned with elaborate tomb reliefs and carved
6
epitaphs. The air feels heavy with the presence of the long-departed.
The narrow stone hall twists and turns, disappearing into darkness.
7 The hall seems to have a slight downwards inclination, and it feels
worn and slippery.
The air here is stale, and the sound of rats scurrying can be heard in
8
the distance.
An eerie silence ills this corridor, broken only by the faint whispers
9
of long-dead spirits. The air is heavy with an otherworldly stillness.
100 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionD20 CORRIDOR DESCRIPTION (CONTINUED)
Strange luminescent fungi cover the walls, casting an ethereal glow.
10 The tunnel twists and turns, creating an unsettling sense of
disorientation.
This wide corridor is lined with towering statues and ornate
11 sarcophagi. Faded tapestries hang from the ceiling, telling stories of
ancient rulers.
The tunnel is partially submerged in water, with small ripples
12
disturbing its still surface. The faint odor of decay permeates the air.
A hidden alcove branches off from the main passage, housing a
13 forgotten altar or a small shrine. Dust-covered offerings lie
undisturbed.
Strange symbols are etched onto the walls of this hall, possibly
14
indicating directions.
The walls of this tunnel bear scorch marks, evidence of past ire or
15 magical explosions. The air carries a faint smell of burnt wood and
stone.
Time and decay have taken their toll on this corridor, with
16
crumbling walls and ceilings. It feels unstable and prone to collapse.
Faint whispers and hushed voices ill the air, voices carried through
17
time.
The stench of decay is overpowering in this tunnel, infested with
18
ravenous vermin that lurk in the shadows.
Intricate carvings adorn the walls, depicting scenes of ancient
19 rituals and funerary rites. The air is heavy with a sense of solemnity
and reverence.
The corridor seems to shimmer with an otherworldly glow.
20 Transparent igures of long-dead souls can be seen drifting through
the walls, oblivious to the living.

A nagging voice rises within you, making you question why are you
37 bothering carrying all those coins when you have no use for them

Unfortunately, the pile seems to be much more unstable than you

damage.
You ind strange markings on the walls and loors. Make a Reason
check: if you pass it you gain 10 XP, as you understand better the
nature of this place and its inhabitants. If you fail, you’re so
39
distracted by the markings that you fail to realize that you’re being

room. Each time you enter this room you must make a Resolve check
to avoid losing 2 Sanity, but you can attempt to Scavenge it twice.

43 of the body. The charm allows you to add +D4 Fire damage to your

If you fail you’ve managed to accidentally touch the blood, which

## Finding Containers

Aside from the Scavenge check
described above, you can choose to
make a deep search once per room
to see if there are any hidden

you do so, you must immediately

116 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionLOCKS & TRAPS
When you encounter a door or it. This process is divided into two
container, it may be locked, phases: Investigation (inding out
trapped, or both. Follow these what’s there) and Resolution
steps to determine the nature of (dealing with it).
the obstacle and how to overcome

## Phase 1: Investigation

Before you attempt to interact with a feature, it is recommended to
investigate it for potential dangers and obstacles. Determine its state: As the
player, irst you need to establish the feature’s status. Make the following
two rolls:
A. Is it trapped? First, you must make a Perception check; the Dificulty of
this check is determined by rolling on the Random Dificulty table.
I. If you passed the Perception check: You must roll D10: a result of
7+ means that the feature is trapped, and you spot it. Roll on the
Traps table to determine which trap you are dealing with. You can
now choose how to deal with it in the Resolution phase 2.A. If there
is no trap, but the door or container is locked, go to 2.B.
II. If you failed the Perception check: You must make a decision: do
you interact with the feature, or do you leave it be? If you interact
with it, roll D10: a result of 7+ means that the feature is trapped,
and you just triggered it. Continue to the Resolution phase.
B. Is it locked? (Doors & Containers only): Roll a D20.
I. Door: A result of 12+ means it is locked.
II. Container: A result of 10+ means it is locked.

## Phase 2: Resolution

Based on what your Investigation revealed (or failed to reveal), you must
now decide how to act.
A. If you detected a trap, you have three options:
I. Disarm it: This requires a set of Thieves’ Tools. Attempt a Thievery
check. The Dificulty of this check is determined by a roll on the
Random Dificulty table.
a. Success: The trap is disabled. You may now deal with any locks
(step B) or interact with the feature safely.
b. Failure: You trigger the trap! Go to C. Trap Triggered.
II. Bypass it (Environmental Traps Only): If the trap is in a room or
corridor, you may try to avoid it completely. Perform the Skill check
to avoid the speciic trap listed on the Traps table. You have +20 to
this check.
a. Success: You safely avoid the trap. You may freely attempt to
exit in any direction (if encountering a door, repeat this
process. Any interactions with said door do NOT trigger the
Chapter IV: Exploring Ker Nethalas 117Environmental Trap); you cannot scavenge or interact with
other objects (other than doors) within the location. Any
future attempts to bypass this same trap are considered
Effortless (+20).
b. Failure: You trigger the trap! Go to C. Trap Triggered.
III. Trigger it: If you decide that you must get rid of a trap, you can
choose to voluntarily trigger it. This grants you +20 to the trap’s
avoidance check.
a. Success: The trap is triggered, but you manage to avoid all
consequences.
b. Failure: You suffer the trap’s consequences, as seen on its
description.
B. If there is a lock (and any traps have been disarmed), you have two
options:
I. Pick the lock: This requires a Lockpick. Attempt a Thievery check.
a. Success: The lock opens.
b. Failure: You fail to open the lock. Your Lockpick breaks, but you
may attempt it again, reducing its Dificulty by one step (e.g.,
from Demanding to Normal, etc.) .
II. Brute force it: You can attempt to break open the door or container.
Attempt an Athletics check.
a. Success: The feature is forced open.
b. Failure: You fail to break it open. You may attempt it again,
increasing your Athletics check by +20 for each attempt after
the irst one.
c. Noise: Regardless of success or failure, each attempt makes a
loud noise. Trigger a Tension Die check.
C. Trap Triggered: If you fail your Perception check, interacting with a
trapped feature will trigger it. This includes:
I. Opening an unlocked feature
II. Attempting to pick the lock
III. Attempting to brute force the feature
IV. Doing anything in a location with an Environmental Trap (consider
a failed Perception check as immediate Triggering)
If you have succeeded in your Perception check, the following still triggers
the trap:
• Failure on Thievery check
• Failure on Bypass check (Environmental Traps only)
• Doing anything in a location with an Environmental Trap other than
exiting via corridor or interacting with a door
• Voluntary Triggering
Other things to take into consideration:
• If a trap triggers as a consequence of a failed Perception check, roll to
determine the trap type, then make a Skill check to avoid it. Success in
this check negates all consequences of the trap.
• If a trap is triggered after a successful Perception check, make a Skill
118 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound Editioncheck to avoid it with +20 modiier due to your awareness. Success
negates all consequences.
• Regardless, traps trigger only once and are considered disarmed from
that moment onward.

## D100 Growing Darkness

A choir of howls ills the air. All Animal opponents receive +10

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 135ABYSSAL VEXATOR
Abyssal Vexators are demons specialized in inlicting excruciating pain upon
their victims. Their monstrous form is dificult to discern, as they are
essentially just ethereal shadows shrouded in an aura of oppressive darkness.
Abyssal Vexators wield an array of ethereal chains that writhe and lash like
serpents, each link infused with a cursed enchantment designed to bind and
torment their prey. In combat, an Abyssal Vexator unleashes a terrifying chain
attack, ensnaring its victims with its spectral links, pulling them closer to
experience the full brunt of its maleicent power. The chains wrap tightly
around their bodies, inlicting an agonizing and unending torment that leaves
the unfortunate souls broken and withered.

## Trait

Frightening

## Level Adaptation

After level 5: +D6 damage
After level 10: +D8 damage, +10 Combat Skill

## D6 Actions

Chain Lash (Physical): The Abyssal Vexator extends its ethereal
chains and makes a deadly attack against a target. On a hit, the target
1-2
takes D6 Piercing damage and must succeed on an Athletics check or
be knocked Prone.
Tormenting Bind: The Abyssal Vexator targets a creature it can see
and attempts to wrap its chains around the creature's body. The target
must succeed on a Dodge check or be Restrained by the chains. At the
3-4 start of the target's turn, it takes D4 Necrotic damage as the chains
constrict and torment it. The Abyssal Vexator doesn't act against a
character while they are affected by this Action; it will either change
target or simply wait for the character to die or free themselves.
Abyssal Wither (Magical): The Abyssal Vexator focuses its power on
a target, who must succeed on an Endurance check or have its vitality
5-6 drained away. On a failed check, the target takes D10 Necrotic damage
and its maximum Health is reduced by the amount of damage taken.
This reduction lasts until the target inishes a Camp Rest.
136 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionAMALGAM
Amalgams are grotesque and nightmarish undead constructs, a horriic
fusion of multiple corpses and dark magic. These abominations are easy to
recognize due to their body swollen and bloated from the sickening union of
lesh and decay. Pieces of dismembered limbs and mismatched body parts jut
out at odd angles, held together by taut, necrotic sinews that seem on the
verge of tearing apart at any moment. The Amalgam's lumbering movements
are slow but unyielding, causing the very ground to tremble beneath its
immense weight.

## D6 Actions

Gruesome Slam (Physical): The Amalgam swings one of its massive
1-2 limbs at a target in a brutal, sweeping motion, dealing D6 Bludgeoning
damage.
Necrotic Burst: The Amalgam's decaying lesh pulses with necrotic
3-4 energy as it releases a burst of dark power. All creatures in the room
must succeed on an Endurance check or take D6 Necrotic damage.
Dismembering Strike (Physical): The Amalgam focuses its assault
on a single target, attempting to tear them apart. It makes a melee
attack against the target, dealing D6 Slashing damage. If the attack
hits, the target must succeed on an Athletics check or be Restrained, as
5-6 the Amalgam's gnarled appendages ensnare them. The target takes 1
Slashing damage at the start of their turns while ensnared. The
Amalgam doesn't act against a character while they are affected by
this Action; it will either change target or simply wait for the character
to die or free themselves.
Chapter V: Combat Encounters 137ARMOR CONSTRUCT
The Vaelorian often bound spirits to suits of armor to achieve the perfect
sentinel, patrolling and seeking out those who dare trespass upon its creator's
domain. Armor Constructs are always an imposing sight, composed of heavy
metal plates interlocked to form the appearance of an armored humanoid
igure. Its surface is adorned with intricate runes and sigils, pulsing with an
ominous glow. With every step, the ground trembles beneath its weight, and
its mighty gauntleted ists can pulverize solid stone with ease.

After Hive Larvae have had their ill and devoured everything in their path,
they nest within the carcasses of their victims, eventually emerging as Hive
Warriors, monstrosities with elongated limbs, sleek exoskeletons, and an
almost construct-like appearance. Possessing heightened senses, ferocious
strength, and deadly agility, they guard their subterranean hive and protect
their queen, a colossal and alien entity.

156 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionHOLLOW SHAMBLER
These creatures of decay are the clear result of Vaelorian experimentation
and their never-ending pursuit of creating new forms of undead. Hollow
Shamblers lack a head, leaving their neck a hollow void; instead they possess
an enormous maw that gapes open in their chest cavity, a grotesque chasm
lined with rows of jagged, razor-sharp teeth. Their body is a mass of tattered,
rotting lesh, capable only of moving with a disjointed gait, their limbs
contorting unnaturally, as if puppeteered by an unseen malevolence. The
creatures' purpose is to consume the souls of the living, drawing them into
their chest maw to join the restless spirits trapped within its tormented form.

razor-sharp pincers and venomous stinger are imbued with necromantic

scholarly circles, as it is said to be the creation of a deranged sorcerer who
sought to conquer death itself, now unleashed upon the world as a relentless

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 159NETHERFIEND
Despite being considered only lesser demons, Netheriends are formidable
opponents, wielding infernal powers, capable of conjuring eldritch lames and
weaving tendrils of corrupted magic to ensnare their prey. Standing barely a
meter tall on cloven hooves, its sinewy, obsidian-black body seems to licker
with a sinister, ethereal glow. Horns curve menacingly from its skull, and its
eyes burn with crimson ire, relecting its insatiable thirst for chaos and
suffering. Sharp, jagged wings sprout from its back, carrying the creature
effortlessly through the shadows, allowing it to strike swiftly and vanish into
the darkness.

enemies in all-consuming lames and summoned irestorms, ensuring nothing
but complete devastation.

162 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionRATKIN MARAUDERS
Standing upright with a hunched posture, these nightmarish beasts have
coarse, mottled fur covering their sinewy and muscular frame, while their
razor-sharp claws and teeth make for formidable natural weapons. Their eyes
gleam with an unsettling intelligence, and can be usually seen draped in
tattered remnants of stolen garments and armor, displaying trophies taken
from their vanquished foes as a gruesome warning to any who dare oppose
them. As the lowest warrior caste in Verrixian society, they are the weakest
members; still, they are highly skilled in guerrilla warfare and ambush tactics,
striking swiftly and vanishing into the shadows, leaving behind a trail of
terror and chaos. Ratkin Marauders are the irst among their kin in merciless
raids, plundering settlements and leaving no corner of civilization safe from
their ravenous plundering.

164 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionREANIMATED HOMUNCULUS
These creatures are a twisted amalgamation of necromantic science and dark
alchemy, the most sacred arts in Vaelorian society. Its form is a grotesque
mockery of life, with stitched-together limbs and patchwork lesh, and its body
emits an aura of necrotic energy, crackling with the volatile essence that
animates it. Reanimated Homunculi are highly unstable, their necromantic
essence making it unpredictable and potentially explosive. In combat, they
don’t really pose a serious threat, due to their slow and clumsy movements. It
is only when they appear to freeze in place that running becomes the wise
choice, as an explosion is sure to follow, obliterating both the Homunculus and
anyone in its vicinity.

devoid of life, glow with an unholy, malevolent light, seeking out the living
with a ravenous hunger. The stench of death and decay precedes its arrival,
warning the unfortunate of its relentless pursuit.

unwary intruders that venture into their domain. This monstrous arthropod
boasts a segmented exoskeleton adorned with an array of jagged spines and

seamlessly with the darkness, rendering it nearly invisible until it is upon its
hapless prey. The creature's fanged mandibles secrete a paralyzing venom,
swiftly incapacitating its victims, which it then carries away to its hidden lair
for a slow and terrifying consumption.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 171UNFETTERED FAMILIAR
Once bound to a sorcerer's will, these extraordinary entities possess an
ethereal and shimmering form, translucent like a wisp, and adorned with
faint, arcane markings that trace the vestiges of their past servitude. Though
no longer bound to a master, all Unfettered Familiars retain an aura of
magical potency, hinting at the knowledge they once acquired in their time of
servitude. They roam the many arcane laboratories found in Ker Nethalas,
consumed by rage and hatred of all physical beings, making them terrible foes
to encounter.

Clad in tattered robes that once boasted intricate patterns and symbols of a
bygone era, these undead spellcasters wield an aura of malevolence and
ancient knowledge. Vaelorian Magi command the forces of death and decay,
having transitioned from live necromancers to undead beings long ago. Their
skeletal hands, often the only thing visible under their robes, bear the mark of
countless rituals and forbidden spells, remnants of a life devoted to unlocking
the secrets of forbidden knowledge.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 173VINEKIN
It is unclear what the Vinekin, a plant-like humanoid species, are doing in Ker
Nethalas. Their forms are a tangle of writhing vines and thorny brambles,
that somehow form the outline of a humanoid shape. They wield dark and
chaotic powers, able to manipulate the corrupted plant life to ensnare and
entrap their prey. They can summon tendrils and thorns to tear apart their
enemies, but they seem to prefer direct physical combat. Many attempts at
communicating with them have been made, but unfortunately they seem to be
only interested in violence.

shadows and cosmic dust, a mesmerizing display of cosmic energy harnessed
by its will. When provoked, the Voidweaver unleashes its formidable void
magic, manipulating dark energies to distort the fabric of reality and tearing
its enemies apart with powerful void rays.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 175VORLEG
The reptilian form of Vorlegs is sleek and well-adapted to its dark and damp
habitat, its scales glistening with an oily sheen that relects the little ambient
light found in the necropolis. The Vorleg's beady eyes gleam with intelligence,
and its sharp fangs are ever ready to deliver a venomous bite. What is truly
dangerous about Vorlegs, though, is their multiple tentacles that cover their
backs, which they can use to strike with uncanny precision and ensnare its
prey in a vice-like grip. The tentacles are lined with barbed hooks and secrete
a paralyzing toxin, rendering victims helpless as the creature moves in for the
kill.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 177WRATH ELEMENTAL
Wrath Elementals are a terrifying fusion of elemental fury and infernal
malevolence, a result of different planes of existence clashing together. Their
forms are an ever-shifting tempest of swirling winds, raging ire, surging
water, and crackling lightning, each element locked in eternal conlict within
its monstrous frame. These fearsome creatures wield both the destructive
might of the elements and the corrupting inluence of demonic power. When
provoked, a Wrath Elemental unleashes a devastating onslaught of elemental
wrath, summoning storms of ire, torrents of water, and bolts of lightning to
annihilate its adversaries. Their demonic nature grants them an innate
resilience against harm, allowing them to withstand attacks that would fell
other lesser beings.

and those who survive must face its terrible scythe.

brave its frozen domain and attempt to outwit its relentless underground
advances.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 191PYROCLASMIC POSSESSOR
This entity of iery possession and smoldering dominance manifests as a
malevolent spirit woven from the very lames of the abyss. Its spectral form is
wreathed in incendiary energies that engulf the surroundings, leaving
scorched footprints in its wake. Its most frightening ability is its power to
invade the minds of its victims, compelling them to enact its destructive will,
while conjuring molten projectiles that erupt from the very earth. The
Pyroclasmic Possessor will force those who face it to wrestle the scalding ires
that threaten to consume all in a pyroclasmic inferno.

Infernal Possession (Magical): The Pyroclasmic Possessor targets a
creature, who must succeed on a Resolve check or be possessed for 2
rounds. While possessed, the creature's actions are controlled by the
1
Pyroclasmic Possessor, who will force the creature to attack its allies,
or itself if there aren’t any. The creature can repeat the Resolve check
at the end of each of its turns, ending the possession on a success.

burst of lame that erupts. Creatures in the area must make a Dodge
2

damage on a successful one.

3 molten projectiles at up to three targets. Each target must make a
Dodge check or suffer 2D6 Fire damage.
Possessed Blaze: The Pyroclasmic Possessor envelops itself in a
4 swirling aura of ire for 2 rounds. Creatures that are engaged in
combat with it gain the Burning condition.
Burning Urge: The Pyroclasmic Possessor ushes a iery command
5
that reverberates across the necropolis. Make a Tension Die check.
Inferno's Grasp: The Pyroclasmic Possessor reaches out with its iery
spectral form to grasp a target. The target must pass an Athletics
6
check or take 2D4 Fire damage and be Restrained by the Pyroclasmic
Possessor until the end of its next turn.
Chapter V: Combat Encounters 193VEILSHIFTER
Veilshifter, the Illusory Archon, is a terrifying creature that commands
illusions and weaves intricate webs of deceit. Its form is ever-changing, a
kaleidoscope of images that confound the senses, and its mastery over illusory
arts casts doubt upon the very fabric of truth. Its manipulation of reality can
transform the battleield into a surreal dreamscape, summoning ephemeral
doppelgangers that mimic its abilities, creating a challenge for those who face
it, as they attempt to decipher the real from the unreal.

## D6 Actions

Illusory Mirage: The Veilshifter creates illusory duplicates of itself
1 and its surroundings. It becomes heavily obscured, and all attacks
targeting it have Disadvantage until the end of its next turn.
Ethereal Distortion (Magical): The Veilshifter distorts reality around
2 it, creating a shifting landscape of illusions, causing all creatures in the
area to become Sickened (20) for D4 rounds.
Reality Flux (Magical): The Veilshifter manipulates reality, causing a
3
torrent of illusions to clash upon a target, dealing 2D6 Arcane damage.
Doppelganger Surge: The Veilshifter summons illusory
doppelgangers that support it in combat. Two doppelgangers appear
near the Veilshifter and use its Illusory Mirage action immediately on
4 themselves. These doppelgangers last until they’ve been struck once,
and only act once per round each. In their turn, a doppelganger makes
a Physical attack against a target, dealing 2D4 Psychic damage if
landing a strike. Only 4 doppelgangers can be active at a time.
Dreamscape Shift (Magical): The Veilshifter warps the battleield
5 into an illusory dreamscape, altering the terrain. All creatures in the
area must succeed on an Athletics check or fall Prone.
Mystic Gaze (Magical): The Veilshifter gazes at a target, causing it to
6 perceive illusory but very harmful threats, suffering 4D6 Arcane
damage.
Chapter V: Combat Encounters 195VENOMCLOUD SIREN
The Venomcloud Siren is an eerie enchantress with a domain over the
deadliest of toxins known to man. Her haunting presence is marked by
billowing clouds of virulent gas that choke the air, and her serpentine
movements conjure venomous projectiles that strike with deadly precision.
She leaves a trail of creeping vines and thorns in her wake that ensnare the
unwary, leaving them to quickly rot away and nurture life in the necropolis.

Chapter V: Combat Encounters 197ZEPHYROUS SHADOW
The Zephyrous Shadow is an enigmatic force woven from the very threads of
lightning and shadow. This Overseer sends bolts of electric fury crackling
through the air, casting curses that shroud its victims in disorienting
darkness, and showing its dominion over the atmospheric energies that allow
it to cast lightning-infused projectiles. Confronting the Zephyrous Shadow
demands an absolute mastery of arms and the ability to unravel its elusive,
thunderous tactics.

206 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionWEAPONS

## Weapon Traits

All non-magical weapons,
regardless of the type, deal D6
damage. They also have a different
Speed value, depending on the
weapon. But most importantly,
every weapon has one or more
properties that set them aside
from the rest called Traits.
• Defensive: +10 combat Skill
when used defensively in
combination with a shield.
• Quick: This weapon grants the
character +10 to Initiative
checks.
• Parrying: Allows you to use
your weapon Skill when
defending against attacks.
• Powerful: This weapon is
especially deadly, increasing
damage by +1.
• Simple: A weapon relatively
easy to use, increasing the
combat Skill by +10.
• Two-Handed: A character
must use two hands to wield
this weapon, but its larger size
increases damage by +2.
• Versatile: When wielded with
two hands, this weapon deals
+1 damage.

## Weapon Weight

All One-Handed weapons are
considered Normal items. Two-
Handed weapons are Heavy items.
As usual though, this only matters
if you’re not currently wielding the
weapon and are carrying it in your
bags instead.

Chapter VI: Spoils & Loot 215D10 LORE BOOKS
Shadows of Devotion: The Vaelorian Rites of Necromantic
Worship.“Through a potent blend of religion and dark sorcery, the
Vaelorians worship the realm of death itself, embracing the forbidden
arts that bind them to the eternal cycle of life and decay. Shadow-clad
priests, adorned in somber vestments, conduct solemn ceremonies to
commune with the departed souls in their hallowed temples. They seek
1
divine favor through rituals shrouded in macabre mystique,
harnessing the power of necromancy to unlock secrets hidden in the
realms beyond mortal comprehension. The Vaelorian Ascendancy's
complete devotion to necromantic practices and their self-imposed
isolation have forged an empire where faith and the manipulation of
death intertwine, forming the very core of their society.”
Aetheric Tailoring. “Our creations are more than mere garments;
they are conduits of enigmatic magic. The fabrics we produce possess
an intrinsic essence, an uncanny enchantment that cloaks the wearer
in an aura of mystique. We weave secrets into the very fabric of our
2
society with each stitch, concealing even the darkest intentions
beneath layers of artistry and illusion. Our work grants those who don
our creations an enigmatic allure, transforming them into igures of
fascination and reverence in any social circle.”
Mysteries of Death. “Kalatha, the Sovereign of Shadows, is revered as
the eternal orchestrator of the cosmic cycle, her skeletal hand guiding
souls from the mortal realm to the veiled mysteries beyond. The
Temple of Ebon Rites stands as a monument to her dominion, its
towering spires piercing the heavens as a irm declaration of our
reverence. There, cloaked acolytes perform their rituals under the dim
glow of obsidian candles, invoking Kalatha's name with whispered
prayers and offerings of bone and ash. The goddess's presence is felt
3 keenly in the rituals of necromancy, where death is both a path to
power and a sacrament of devotion. Yet, Kalatha's blessings are never
given freely; they must be earned through loyalty and sacriice. Her
worshipers navigate the delicate balance between life and death,
embracing the inevitability of their mortality while wielding the
potency of necromantic arts. As the Vaelorian Ascendancy's guiding
light, Kalatha bestows a chilling solace upon her devoted, a promise of
unity with the shadows that shroud our world in an absolute
embrace.”
216 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionD10 LORE BOOKS (CONTINUED)
Contemplations on the Tapestry of Realms. “Dear minds curious
enough to delve into the labyrinthine corridors of thought, I present to
you the heart of my contemplations in this humble tome you hold in
your hands. In its pages, I dare to explore the intricate origins of the
diverse planes of existence, those realms veiled from our senses yet
profoundly interwoven with our reality. Imagine, if you will, a celestial
loom, where primordial weavers spin the threads of existence. Each
4 plane, distinct and evocative, is its own micro-universe: a realm of ire,
another of water, one of shadow, and countless others. These parallel
domains intersect with our world, like ethereal echoes casting
intricate patterns upon our reality. My musings, though bold and met
with skepticism, invite you to transcend the ordinary and embrace the
enigma that our reality weaves. Let us embark together on this voyage
of understanding, peering beyond the veil to fathom the cosmic loom
that shapes the very essence of our being.”
The Battle of Curum. “The Battle of Curum, a chapter seared into the
living memory of the Vaelorian Ascendancy, unfolds in my mind like a
visceral painting of heroism and strife. There, on the rain-soaked
battleground, our shadowsteel-clad warriors clashed with the
invading Ashthreni horde, an unwavering bulwark against
encroaching chaos. The Obsidian Veil, like spectral wraiths, cut
5
through enemy ranks, their blades weaving a symphony of death. The
Inquisitors' incantations shook the very ground in that tempest of
combat, their necromantic might turning the tide. Victory was earned
through blood and valor, proving once again our empire's unyielding
spirit and the triumph of the Vaelorian Ascendancy against the
relentless storm.”
The Midnight Throne. “In the heart of the Vaelorian Ascendancy
stands Ker Nethalas, the Midnight Throne, a city unrivaled in its
grandeur and mystique. To wander through its labyrinthine alleys is
to traverse the crossroads of divine devotion and architectural marvel.
The obsidian spires, etched with arcane symbols, reach skyward as if
6 seeking the touch of the gods. The very stones beneath one's feet
whisper the secrets of generations, and the air is alive with a palpable
energy, embodying our history and unyielding faith. The Midnight
Throne is more than just a capital, it's a living testament to our
empire's soul, a manifestation of the shadows that cradle us, and a
sentinel guarding the holiness of the Vaelorian Ascendancy.”
Chapter VI: Spoils & Loot 217D10 LORE BOOKS (CONTINUED)
A Thousand Years of Glory. “In the annals of the Vaelorian
Ascendancy's saga, one name emerges as a symbol of authority and
enigma: Emperor Aurelius the Undying. Like a igure conjured from
the depths of our collective consciousness, Aurelius ruled with an iron
ist sheathed in velvet gloves, his every decree reverberating across
our history like the whispers of fate itself. It is said that he possessed
an aura that bridged the mortal and the divine, embodying the power
7
of the Ascendancy's theocratic might. Yet, it was not just his dominion
that earned him the epithet 'the Undying'. Rather, it was his absolute
connection to the necromantic arts, a mastery that stirred both awe
and trepidation. In all of our extensive history, Emperor Aurelius
emerges as a pivotal igure that served as a mortal conduit to the
gods' divine will, cementing and guarding the secrets of the Vaelorian
Ascendancy's legacy.”
Rising Shadows: A History of House Tesir. “As if ordained by the
very weavers of destiny, this once-obscure lineage ascended the rungs
of power with a grace that belied their origins. From the shadows of
the common quarters, House Tesir's ascent mirrored the empire's own
journey, proof of the validity of the meritocratic ideals embedded in
8 Vaelorian society. Yet, their climb was not bereft of critique; whispers
of clandestine alliances and esoteric bargains linger still in the air.
Under the leadership of Lord Valerius Tesir, the family's inluence
expanded like ripples in a pond. It is said that their mastery over the
arts of assassination became the foundation of their dominion,
intertwining their name with both reverence and fear.”
The Third Path. “The convergence of necromancy and demonology,
like the crossing of two shadowy rivers, invokes a dichotomy that both
intrigues and unsettles. But the marriage of necromancy's communion
with the departed and demonology's conjuration of infernal entities
creates a well of forbidden potential; what secrets are whispered by
the abyssal entities drawn from the nether realms? The fusion of these
9
magics has sparked both fascination and condemnation, blurring the
lines between mastery and recklessness. It is true that our work on
these dark arts, a relection of our ceaseless ambition, serves often as
a cautionary tale of the relentless pursuit of dominion over the arcane,
but I dare to claim that any risks are insigniicant, compared to the
power offered by this Third Path.”
218 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionD10 LORE BOOKS (CONTINUED)
The Ganeus Rebellion. “In the intricate mosaic of the Vaelorian
Ascendancy's history, the treacherous saga of House Ganeus is one
that, to this day, still casts a shadow that lingers as a reminder of the
precarious nature of power and loyalty. Once esteemed pillars of the
empire, their name now resonates as a byword for betrayal. The web
of deceit they wove, concealed beneath a veneer of allegiance, speaks
to the abyss that can form between kin and empire. It is said that
House Ganeus courted forbidden pacts with entities beyond mortal
10
ken, seeking power that transcended the very bounds of our world.
Their duplicity unfurled like a venomous serpent, poisoning the very
heart of our Ascendancy. The Ganeus Rebellion, an event etched in
sorrow and shame, saw the empire's own children raising arms
against it, their actions seared with the stain of peridy. The tale of
House Ganeus is an everlasting reminder that shadows can be cast
from within as much as from without, and that we must always be
wary of our own hubris and desire for power.”

## Magic Item Generation Procedure

• Roll on the Item table to learn which type of item you’ve found.
• Roll on the Item Rarity table to learn how many Properties it has.
• Roll as many times as necessary on the corresponding item table
to learn the details of the item’s powers. If you roll twice on the
same Property the effects stack.
• The only exception to this procedure are Amulets. When you ind
an Amulet, simply roll once on the Random Mastery table (page
222) to learn which Mastery it grants you access to.
• To speed up the process you can make all the rolls at once: simply
grab one D8, one D10 and one D20. If the item turns out to be of
Rare or Epic rarity, re-roll the D20 as many times as necessary.

Chapter VI: Spoils & Loot 233RELICS
Relics are powerful, unique magic
items that grant you access to one
of a kind powers, abilities and
advantages. Finding one of these
items can change how you play the
game, and really change the course
of your character’s fate.
Characters do not need to attune
with Relics, and as such are able to
use them as soon as they acquire
them.

234 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionA   E S
Item Type: Shield (Full)
Increases your Parry by +20
Once per room you can ignore all damage from a single source
The Aegis of the Eternal Sentinel absorbs and disperses incoming attacks with
remarkable eficiency, offering a stalwart defense against both mundane and
magical assaults. The shield's surface is etched with intricate patterns that
evoke the image of a vigilant sentinel, and is hewn from sturdy ironwood
inlaid with veins of luminous crystal. As it is raised, an invisible barrier forms,
embodying the spirit of its namesake.
B  K
Item Type: Ring
After a Camp Rest, choose a Skill: it gains +50 until your next Camp Rest.
These points may not be reallocated until your next Camp Rest
Forged in the heart of the lost library of Kizanu and infused with the essence
of countless tomes, this ring serves as a conduit to unparalleled knowledge.
When slipped onto the inger, it ignites a profound awakening within the
wearer's mind, expanding their intellect and awareness.
D  A P
Item Type: Head Piece
+10 max Aether
All Abilities that deal damage deal an additional +D4 damage
When donned, this delicately crafted diadem made of a single piece of
unidentiied crystal enhances the caster's connection to the arcane,
intensifying the power and eficacy of their spells. Each incantation resonates
with the wearer's intent and ampliies their magical resonance.
D  V
Item Type: Consumable
Permanently increases your Health by +D4
Contained within a murky glass vial is a liquid blend of rare herbs, gemstone
fragments, and distilled essence of vitality itself. When consumed, the potion
surges through the consumer's veins, bolstering their constitution and
fortitude to new levels. As the elixir infuses their being, it mends wounds,
strengthens sinew, and breathes newfound vigor into the very core of their
existence.
Chapter VI: Spoils & Loot 235E  R
Item Type: Trinket
If one blow were to kill you, ignore all damage and heal D10 Health
instead. The Echo of Resurgence shatters afterwards
A product of intricate enchantments woven into a fragile crystal, this trinket
hangs from a slender chain, resting gently against the wearer's heart. A
single, precious irreplaceable gift is bestowed upon the bearer: a second
chance at life. When faced with imminent demise, the trinket's magic
awakens, casting a protective cocoon around the wearer and absorbing the
fatal blow.
E  A A
Item Type: Consumable
Permanently increases your Aether by D4
This vial of shimmering luminescence radiates an aura of magical
empowerment. When imbibed, the Elixir of Arcane Ascendance awakens a
dormant wellspring of arcane potential within the consumer, elevating their
casting abilities to new heights. As the elixir courses through their veins, the
bearer's body becomes more in tune with the aetheric planes, allowing them
to tap into arcane energies with ease.
E R B
Item Type: Scimitar
Attacks with this weapon ignore Armor
You can never drop this weapon unwillingly
Recover 1 Sanity after a successful Combat Encounter
Forged from an alloy of silvered steel and infused with fragments of star
metal, the sword's blade curves with an ethereal grace. Intricate runes
intertwine across its hilt, and when unsheathed, the blade hums with a
melancholic resonance, its touch exuding an aura of spectral cold. With each
swing, the sword sings a silent lament, its strikes capable of bypassing both
armor and lesh to wound the very essence of a foe.
G S
Item Type: Consumable
+1 Ability Point
When ingested, this ebony-hued seed merges seamlessly with the essence of
the consumer, awakening an innate potential within. As tendrils of shadow
curl through their veins, a surge of newfound strength courses through their
body, bestowing an increase in power.
236 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionG   B P
Item Type: Gloves
You’re Resistant to Fire damage
Once per Camp Rest you can use the Flamecaster’s Tier 4 Summon Fire
Elemental Ability. The elemental disappears when you exit the room
These gloves woven from enchanted crimson silk and adorned with intricate
patterns resembling dancing lames it snugly against the wearer's hands,
imbuing them with the power to command ire. Upon activating their magic,
the wearer can summon a iery elemental ally, a creature born of the very
lames they seek to control.
I P
Item Type: Plate (Torso)
This piece of armor bursts into lames when you engage in combat, dealing
1 Fire damage per round to all opponents
Crafted from enchanted obsidian and etched with intricate lame motifs by
the enslaved Orneka blacksmiths, this plate rests upon the wearer's shoulders
like a mantle of infernal power. Upon entering combat, the armor awakens,
erupting into majestic lames that dance along its surface, warding off
assailants with an aura of searing heat. As the wearer wades into battle, their
enemies are confronted with the choice of engaging in close combat and
facing the blistering lames or keeping a cautious distance.
M  E
Item Type: Mace
+2 Force damage
Any creature struck by this mace must pass an Endurance check or be
Dazed (1)
Bound with leather straps and accented by blackened steel, the mace's head is
sculpted into a wickedly spiked orb, etched with intricate lunar motifs. When
swung, the Mace of Eclipse radiates an aura of palpable darkness, its strikes
carrying an otherworldly chill that engulfs enemies in an umbra of dread,
weakening their resolve and sapping their strength.
M E
Item Type: Consumable
Permanently increases your Sanity by D4
Comprising a harmonious blend of rare herbs, moonlit dew, and crystalline
extracts, this elixir embodies the essence of serenity and mental resilience.
When imbibed, the Mindward Elixir envelops the consumer's psyche in a
soothing embrace, fortifying their mental defenses against psychic assaults
and providing solace against inner turmoils.
Chapter VI: Spoils & Loot 237P  F F
Item Type: Consumable
Upon drinking this Potion, you automatically lose access to one of your
two starting Masteries. You are free to pick a new one, assigning the same
number of Ability Points that were assigned to the forgotten Mastery.
This swirling black liquid slushes and bubbles as if it has a mind of its own. A
person drinking this mysterious liquid feels the brew eagerly sliding into their
throat, choking them. After a minute of pure agony and being unable to
breathe, they vomit a black crystal containing all their old power and
experiences, and feel an empty space in their soul eager to be illed with new
power.
R  A T
Item Type: Ring
Allows the character to blast a wave of aetheric energies that deal 2D10
Arcane damage to all opponents in the room. This effect can be activated
once per Camp Rest
Fashioned from a lustrous blend of obsidian and sapphire, the ring's surface is
etched with intricate glyphs that pulse in rhythm with the surrounding arcane
currents. When worn, it grants the wearer the ability to unleash a devastating
wave of raw arcane power against their adversaries.
R  E G
Item Type: Ring
Grants the character 5 Aether that can only be spent on summoning
Minions.
Crafted from a rare fusion of argent metal and a crystalline core, the ring
pulsates with energy that resonates with the wearer's desire for dominion.
Once worn, it unlocks the capacity to command an increased number of
minions, forging an unbreakable psychic link that ampliies their reach and
inluence. As the wearer's will extends over the augmented horde, a fraction of
their consciousness melds with their minions, bridging the chasm between
individuality and collective purpose.
R  S S
Item Type: Ring
Increases the Health of Tier 1 Minions by ⅃/2
This twisted emblem of dominion exudes an aura of enigmatic control. When
worn by its master, this obsidian ring surges with dark energy, resonating with
the dark pact it embodies. It empowers the wearer's minions, elevating their
potency and loyalty.
238 Ker Nethalas - Gravebound EditionT E  E
Item Type: Trinket
You have Advantage on all Perception and Scavenging checks
You can never be surprised
Hewn from a rare onyx infused with a glimmering sapphire pupil, this jewel is
fashioned in the shape of a vigilant eye, its gaze eternal and all-encompassing.
When held, the Third Eye of Emaricus allows the bearer an unparalleled level of
awareness, becoming virtually impossible to surprise. Their gaze sees it all and
everyone, even in the almost absolute darkness of Ker Nethalas.
V C
Item Type: Belt
Increase your Health by 5
You gain access to the Hexmancer’s Tier 4 Ability Drain Lifeforce
Fashioned from supple leather and woven with threads of life-afirming
magic, this belt encircles the waist with a reassuring weight. Upon donning it,
a surge of newfound stamina courses through the wearer's veins, bolstering
their constitution and fortitude. In times of dire need, the belt's latent power
can be harnessed, enabling the wearer to siphon the life force of foes through
a touch, replenishing their own health while sapping the vitality of their
adversaries.
V G
Item Type: Fist weapon
Target receives a minimum of 1 damage every time you successfully strike
them, regardless of Armor or resistances
Once per Combat Encounter you may spend a Reaction to turn your
opponent’s attack against them, leaving you unharmed
Engraved sigils shimmer with a faint iridescence across these black, supple
gauntlets, invoking the power of the void. The Voidclasp Gauntlets channel the
power of the void into the wearer's strikes, infusing them with a dark energy
that rends both armor and lesh alike.
W
Item Type: Spear
+D6 Lightning damage
Reduce the opponent’s Attack Skill by 5 each time you deal damage to
them
Forged from a fusion of mithril and wyrm's fang, the spear's blade gleams
with uncontained luminosity, evoking the power of both heaven and
dragonkind, and etched along its shaft are intricate runes that pulse with
radiant energy. As it is wielded, Wyrmfang burns with an inner ire,
channeling the bearer's strength into piercing strikes that carry the fervor of
celestial wrath.

Item Type: Gear (Heavy)
A character in possession of the Diary can keep track of a third Personal
Goal
This plain looking, completely empty book somehow compels you to write in

B S
Item Type: Amulet
A character in possession of a Borestian Stone can immediately identify
Potions without requiring a Medicine check
These small red, polished stones are among the most sought-after items in
Ker Nethalas, the reason why alchemists send their apprentices to an almost
guaranteed death into the tunnels of the necropolis. No matter how much
modern-day sorcerers and alchemists have tried replicating them, nobody
has ever achieved it, and the secret of their making is one the Vaelorian
Magi took with them to their graves.

A mummiied yet still beating heart, the size of an apple. It is said it contains
some of the power of the dreaded Amal-Tul, sworn enemy of the Vaelorian
Ascendancy. After his capture at the hands of The Black Legion, several of
his body parts were transformed into trinkets, or preserved magically.

This grotesque looking mace, made of what appears to be some sort of bone

wounds that manifest like rends on the target’s lesh, with the blood
becoming instantly crystalized and shattering against the loor.
